1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the holomorphic space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 121 and the convergence rate is O(206/n^2).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on holomorphic structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 121. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 206. QED.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^489(S, F) is trivial. QED.
